                                              Avoid unused values (MISRAC2025-RULE_2_2-b-2) 
                                           [8/8]  Rule 2.1 (Required) A project shall not contain unreachable code (MISRAC2025-RULE_2_1)

                                              There shall be no unreachable code in "if/else/while/for" block (MISRAC2025-RULE_2_1-c-2) 
                                              There shall be no unreachable code in switch statement (MISRAC2025-RULE_2_1-d-2) 
                                              There shall be no unreachable code in 'for' loop (MISRAC2025-RULE_2_1-e-2) 
                                              There shall be no unreachable code after 'if' or 'switch' statement (MISRAC2025-RULE_2_1-f-2) 
                                              There shall be no unreachable code after "if" or "switch" statement inside while/for/do...while loop (MISRAC2025-RULE_2_1-g-2) 
                                              Avoid unreachable methods (MISRAC2025-RULE_2_1-h-2)
